<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Casts;

use App\Support\PersianNormalizer;
use Carbon\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Database\Eloquent\CastsAttributes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Morilog\Jalali\Jalalian;
use Throwable;

class JalaliDateCast implements CastsAttributes
{
    public function get(Model $model, string $key, mixed $value, array $attributes): ?Carbon
    {
        if (! $value) {
            return null;
        }

        return Carbon::parse($value)->timezone('Asia/Tehran');
    }

    public function set(Model $model, string $key, mixed $value, array $attributes): ?string
    {
        return self::toGregorian($value);
    }

    public static function toGregorian(mixed $value): ?string
    {
        if ($value === null || $value === '') {
            return null;
        }

        if ($value instanceof Carbon || $value instanceof \DateTimeInterface) {
            return Carbon::instance($value)->format('Y-m-d');
        }

        $normalized = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText((string) $value);
        $normalized = str_replace('-', '/', $normalized);

        if (preg_match('/^1[34]\d{2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}$/', $normalized)) {
            $parts = array_map('intval', explode('/', $normalized));

            return (new Jalalian($parts[0], $parts[1], $parts[2]))->toCarbon()->format('Y-m-d');
        }

        try {
            return Carbon::parse($normalized)->format('Y-m-d');
        } catch (Throwable) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static function toJalali(mixed $date, string $format = 'Y/m/d'): string
    {
        if (! $date) {
            return 'â€”';
        }

        try {
            if (is_string($date)) {
                $normalized = str_replace('-', '/', PersianNormalizer::normalizeText($date));
                if (preg_match('/^1[34]\d{2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}$/', $normalized)) {
                    return $normalized;
                }
            }

            return Jalalian::fromCarbon(Carbon::parse($date)->timezone('Asia/Tehran'))->format($format);
        } catch (Throwable) {
            return (string) $date;
        }
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\SystemActivity;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\File;
use Throwable;

class BackupDatabaseCommand extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'bimeh:backup-database';

    protected $description = 'Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†â€ŒÚ¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ùˆ ÙØ´Ø±Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø² Ø¯ÛŒØªØ§Ø¨ÛŒØ³ Ø¯Ø± Ù…Ø³ÛŒØ± Ø§Ù…Ù† storage/app/private/backups';

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $backupDir = storage_path('app/private/backups');
        if (! File::isDirectory($backupDir)) {
            File::makeDirectory($backupDir, 0755, true);
        }

        $timestamp = now('Asia/Tehran')->format('Y-m-d_H-i-s');
        $fileName = "backup_afagh_bimeh_{$timestamp}.sql.gz";
        $filePath = $backupDir . DIRECTORY_SEPARATOR . $fileName;

        try {
            $pdo = DB::connection()->getPdo();
            $sql = "-- Afagh Bimeh Automated Database Backup\n";
            $sql .= "-- Date (Asia/Tehran): " . now('Asia/Tehran')->toDateTimeString() . "\n";
            $sql .= "SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=0;\n\n";

            $tables = DB::select('SHOW TABLES');
            foreach ($tables as $tableObj) {
                $table = (string) array_values((array) $tableObj)[0];

                $createTableResult = DB::select("SHOW CREATE TABLE `{$table}`");
                $createSyntax = (array) ($createTableResult[0] ?? []);
                $createSql = $createSyntax['Create Table'] ?? array_values($createSyntax)[1] ?? '';

                if ($createSql !== '') {
                    $sql .= "DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `{$table}`;\n{$createSql};\n\n";
                }

                foreach (DB::table($table)->cursor() as $row) {
                    $rowArray = (array) $row;
                    $columns = array_map(fn ($col) => "`{$col}`", array_keys($rowArray));
                    $values = array_map(function ($val) use ($pdo) {
                        if ($val === null) {
                            return 'NULL';
                        }
                        return $pdo->quote((string) $val);
                    }, array_values($rowArray));

                    $sql .= sprintf(
                        "INSERT INTO `%s` (%s) VALUES (%s);\n",
                        $table,
                        implode(', ', $columns),
                        implode(', ', $values)
                    );
                }
                $sql .= "\n";
            }

            $sql .= "SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=1;\n";

            $compressed = gzencode($sql, 9);
            File::put($filePath, $compressed !== false ? $compressed : $sql);

            // Ù†Ú¯Ù‡Ø¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ú©Ø«Ø± Û±Û´ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø¨Ú©Ø§Ù¾ Ø¢Ø®Ø± Ùˆ Ø­Ø°Ù Ù‚Ø¯ÛŒÙ…ÛŒâ€ŒØªØ±Ù‡Ø§
            $files = collect(File::files($backupDir))
                ->sortByDesc(fn (\SplFileInfo $file) => $file->getMTime())
                ->values();

            if ($files->count() > 14) {
                foreach ($files->slice(14) as $oldFile) {
                    File::delete($oldFile->getPathname());
                }
            }

            SystemActivity::create([
                'event_type' => 'cron_job_executed',
                'title'      => 'Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†â€ŒÚ¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø¯ÛŒØªØ§Ø¨ÛŒØ³ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø§Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ù… Ø´Ø¯',
                'icon'       => 'app_reports',
                'color'      => 'emerald',
                'details'    => [
                    'description' => "ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†: {$fileName}",
                ],
            ]);

            $this->info("ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù† Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ø´Ø¯: {$fileName}");

            return self::SUCCESS;
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            $this->error('Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†â€ŒÚ¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ Ø¯ÛŒØªØ§Ø¨ÛŒØ³: ' . $e->getMessage());

            return self::FAILURE;
        }
    }
}

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Installment;
use App\Models\SystemActivity;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class MarkOverdueInstallments extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'bimeh:mark-overdue';

    protected $description = 'ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø· Ø³Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒØ¯ Ú¯Ø°Ø´ØªÙ‡ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚ (overdue) Ø¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Ø§Ø³ Asia/Tehran';

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $todayTehran = now('Asia/Tehran')->toDateString();
        $updatedCount = 0;

        Installment::with('policy.user')
            ->where('status', 'pending')
            ->whereDate('due_date', '<', $todayTehran)
            ->chunkById(200, function ($installments) use (&$updatedCount): void {
                foreach ($installments as $installment) {
                    $installment->update(['status' => 'overdue']);
                    $updatedCount++;
                }
            });

        if ($updatedCount > 0) {
            SystemActivity::create([
                'event_type' => 'cron_job_executed',
                'title'      => "ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª {$updatedCount} Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ø³Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒØ¯ Ú¯Ø°Ø´ØªÙ‡ Ø¨Ù‡ Â«Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚Â»",

                    'description' => "ØªØ¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯ {$updatedCount} Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ø¯Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø³Ø§Ø¹Øª Û°Û±:Û³Û° Ø¨Ø§Ù…Ø¯Ø§Ø¯ Ø¨Ù‡ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚ Ø¯Ø±Ø¢Ù…Ø¯.",

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Casts\JalaliDateCast;
use App\Enums\InstallmentStatus;
use App\Models\Installment;
use App\Models\NotificationSetting;
use App\Models\SystemActivity;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Services\NotificationDispatcherService;
use Carbon\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class SendInstallmentReminders extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'bimeh:send-installment-reminders';

    protected $description = 'Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø³Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒØ¯ØŒ Ø¯ÛŒØ±Ú©Ø±Ø¯ Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø· Ùˆ ØªØ¨Ø±ÛŒÚ© ØªÙˆÙ„Ø¯ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø±Ø§Ù† (Ø¯Ø±ÙˆÙ†â€ŒØ¨Ø±Ù†Ø§Ù…Ù‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ + Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù„Ù‡)';

    public function handle(NotificationDispatcherService $dispatcher): int
    {
        $settings = NotificationSetting::current();
        $today = Carbon::now('Asia/Tehran')->startOfDay();
        $offsets = array_map('intval', $settings->reminder_offsets ?: [-3, 0, 2, 7]);

        $sentCount = 0;
        $birthdayCount = 0;

        // Û±. Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù‡ÙˆØ´Ù…Ù†Ø¯ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± Ø³Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒØ¯ Ùˆ Ø§Ø®Ø·Ø§Ø± Ø¯ÛŒØ±Ú©Ø±Ø¯ Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø·
        Installment::with(['policy.user'])
            ->whereIn('status', [InstallmentStatus::Pending->value, InstallmentStatus::Overdue->value])
            ->whereNotNull('due_date')
            ->chunkById(200, function ($installments) use ($dispatcher, $settings, $today, $offsets, &$sentCount): void {
                foreach ($installments as $installment) {
                    $user = $installment->policy?->user;
                    if (! $user || ! $installment->due_date) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $diffDays = (int) $installment->due_date->copy()->startOfDay()->diffInDays($today, false);

                    $effectiveOffsets = collect($offsets)->map(function (int $offset) use ($user, $settings): int {
                        if ($offset < 0 && $settings->adaptive_timing_enabled) {
                            return $user->getAdaptiveFirstReminderOffset($offset);
                        }

                        return $offset;
                    })->unique()->values()->all();

                    if (! in_array($diffDays, $effectiveOffsets, true)) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $template = $diffDays > 0 ? $settings->overdue_template : $settings->installment_template;
                    $signedUrl = $installment->generateSignedPaymentUrl();

                    $message = $dispatcher->formatTemplate($template, [
                        'name'          => $user->name ?: $user->mobile,
                        'policy_number' => $installment->policy->policy_number,
                        'amount'        => number_format((int) $installment->amount),
                        'due_date'      => $installment->due_date_jalali,
                        'payment_url'   => $signedUrl,
                    ]);

                    $log = $dispatcher->dispatch(
                        $user,
                        'installment_reminder',
                        (int) $installment->id,
                        $diffDays,
                        $message,
                        $signedUrl
                    );

                    if ($log) {
                        $sentCount++;
                    }
                }
            });

        // Û². Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± ØªØ¨Ø±ÛŒÚ© ØªÙˆÙ„Ø¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…ØªÙˆÙ„Ø¯ÛŒÙ† Ø§Ù…Ø±ÙˆØ²
        if ($settings->birthday_greeting_enabled && ! empty($settings->birthday_template)) {
            $todayJalaliMonthDay = JalaliDateCast::toJalali($today, 'm/d');
            $todayGregorianMonthDay = $today->format('m-d');
            $currentYear = (int) JalaliDateCast::toJalali($today, 'Y');

            User::whereNotNull('birth_date')
                ->where('birth_date', '!=', '')
                ->chunkById(200, function ($users) use ($dispatcher, $settings, $todayJalaliMonthDay, $todayGregorianMonthDay, $currentYear, &$birthdayCount): void {
                    foreach ($users as $user) {
                        $birthStr = str_replace('-', '/', (string) $user->birth_date);
                        $isBirthday = str_ends_with($birthStr, $todayJalaliMonthDay)
                            || str_ends_with((string) $user->birth_date, $todayGregorianMonthDay);

                        if (! $isBirthday) {
                            continue;
                        }

                        $msg = $dispatcher->formatTemplate($settings->birthday_template, [
                            'name' => $user->name ?: 'Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø± Ú¯Ø±Ø§Ù…ÛŒ',
                        ]);

                        $log = $dispatcher->dispatch(
                            $user,
                            'birthday_greeting',
                            (int) $user->id,
                            $currentYear,
                            $msg,
                            route('customer.dashboard')
                        );

                        if ($log) {
                            $birthdayCount++;
                        }
                    }
                });
        }

        if ($sentCount > 0 || $birthdayCount > 0) {
            SystemActivity::create([
                'event_type' => 'cron_job_executed',
                'title'      => "Ø§Ø¬Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ù…ÙˆØªÙˆØ± Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù†: {$sentCount} ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ùˆ {$birthdayCount} ØªØ¨Ø±ÛŒÚ© ØªÙˆÙ„Ø¯",
                'icon'       => 'app_notifications',
                'color'      => 'indigo',
                'details'    => [
                    'description' => "ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± Ù‚Ø³Ø·/Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚Ø§Øª: {$sentCount} Ù…ÙˆØ±Ø¯ | ØªØ¨Ø±ÛŒÚ© ØªÙˆÙ„Ø¯: {$birthdayCount} Ù…ÙˆØ±Ø¯",
                ],

        $this->info("ØªØ¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯ {$sentCount} ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ùˆ {$birthdayCount} ØªØ¨Ø±ÛŒÚ© ØªÙˆÙ„Ø¯ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª ØµÙâ€ŒØ¨Ù†Ø¯ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯.");

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\NotificationSetting;
use App\Models\Policy;
use App\Services\NotificationDispatcherService;
use Carbon\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class SendPolicyRenewalReminders extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'bimeh:send-renewal-reminders';

    protected $description = 'Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± ØªÙ…Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ Ø§Ø² Ù¾Ø§ÛŒØ§Ù† Ø§Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø§Ø± Ø¨Ø§ Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø²Ø´ Ø¯Ø³ØªÙ‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ';

    public function handle(NotificationDispatcherService $dispatcher): int
    {
        $settings = NotificationSetting::current();
        $daysBefore = (int) ($settings->renewal_days_before ?: 15);
        $targetDate = Carbon::now('Asia/Tehran')->addDays($daysBefore)->toDateString();

        $sentCount = 0;

        Policy::with('user')
            ->whereNotNull('expires_at')
            ->whereDate('expires_at', $targetDate)
            ->chunkById(200, function ($policies) use ($dispatcher, $settings, $daysBefore, &$sentCount): void {
                foreach ($policies as $policy) {
                    if (! $policy->user) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    $message = $dispatcher->formatTemplate($settings->renewal_template, [
                        'name'          => $policy->user->name ?: $policy->user->mobile,
                        'policy_number' => $policy->policy_number,
                        'amount'        => number_format((int) $policy->total_amount),
                        'due_date'      => $policy->expires_at_jalali,
                    ]);

                    $log = $dispatcher->dispatch(
                        $policy->user,
                        'policy_renewal',
                        $policy->id,
                        -$daysBefore,
                        $message,
                        route('customer.dashboard')
                    );

                    if ($log) {
                        $sentCount++;
                    }
                }
            });

        $this->info("ØªØ¹Ø¯Ø§Ø¯ {$sentCount} ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± ØªÙ…Ø¯ÛŒØ¯ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯.");

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Enums;

enum InstallmentStatus: string
{
    case Pending = 'pending';
    case AwaitingApproval = 'awaiting_approval';
    case Paid = 'paid';
    case Overdue = 'overdue';

    public function label(): string
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::Pending => 'Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø± Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª',
            self::AwaitingApproval => 'Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø± ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ø¯Ù…ÛŒÙ†',
            self::Paid => 'ÙˆØµÙˆÙ„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡',
            self::Overdue => 'Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚ (Ø³Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒØ¯ Ú¯Ø°Ø´ØªÙ‡)',
        };
    }

    public function color(): string
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::Pending => 'warning',
            self::AwaitingApproval => 'info',
            self::Paid => 'success',
            self::Overdue => 'danger',
        };
    }
}

FILE: D:\گام دوم_ متصل به ربات بله\bimeh-app\app\EnvKit\Client.php

<?php

/**
 * EnvKit debug collector â€” transport shared by the Laravel provider and the
 * Symfony subscriber. Buffers captured signals in memory and flushes them to the
 * EnvKit debug-ingest server AFTER the response is sent (fastcgi_finish_request),
 * so a request being debugged pays ~zero added latency. Entirely dependency-free
 * (raw curl/stream), reads its per-project toggles from the EnvKit state file.
 *
 * This file is copied verbatim into the project by EnvKit (App\EnvKit namespace),
 * so it MUST stay self-contained.
 */

namespace App\EnvKit;

final class Client
{
    /** @var array<int,array<string,mixed>> */
    private array $buffer = [];

    private bool $registered = false;

    private string $requestId;

    /** Request line ("GET /orders/42") tagged onto every event; null for CLI. */
    private ?string $request = null;

    /** @var array<string,mixed>|null */
    private ?array $state = null;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $site = '',
    ) {
        $this->requestId = bin2hex(random_bytes(8));
    }

    /** Resolve EnvKit's per-project state (enabled, passthrough, slowMs, â€¦). */
    public function state(): array
    {
        if ($this->state !== null) {
            return $this->state;
        }
        $dir = getenv('ENVKIT_DEBUG_DIR') ?: '';
        $defaults = [
            'enabled' => false,
            'passthrough' => false,
            'slowMs' => 100,
            'captureWorkers' => false,
            'site' => $this->site,
        ];
        if ($dir === '') {
            return $this->state = $defaults;
        }
        // Key MUST match the TS rootKey(): sha1 of the lowercased, slash-normalized
        // root, first 16 hex chars.
        $root = function_exists('base_path') ? base_path() : getcwd();
        $norm = strtolower(rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', (string) $root), '/'));
        $key = substr(sha1($norm), 0, 16);
        $file = rtrim($dir, '/\\') . '/' . $key . '.json';
        $data = @file_get_contents($file);
        if ($data !== false) {
            $parsed = json_decode($data, true);
            if (is_array($parsed)) {
                return $this->state = array_merge($defaults, $parsed);
            }
        }

        return $this->state = $defaults;
    }

    public function enabled(): bool
    {
        return (bool) ($this->state()['enabled'] ?? false)
            && (getenv('ENVKIT_DEBUG_INGEST') !== false);
    }

    public function passthrough(): bool
    {
        return (bool) ($this->state()['passthrough'] ?? false);
    }

    public function slowMs(): int
    {
        return (int) ($this->state()['slowMs'] ?? 100);
    }

    public function captureWorkers(): bool
    {
        return (bool) ($this->state()['captureWorkers'] ?? false);
    }

    public function requestId(): string
    {
        return $this->requestId;
    }

    /** Set the request line tagged onto subsequent events (web requests only). */
    public function setRequest(string $request): void
    {
        $this->request = $request;
    }

    /** Queue one event; lazily wires the after-response flush on first use. */
    public function record(string $type, array $payload): void
    {
        $payload['type'] = $type;
        $payload['requestId'] = $this->requestId;
        if ($this->request !== null) {
            $payload['request'] = $this->request;
        }
        if (PHP_SAPI === 'cli') {
            $payload['worker'] = true;
        }
        $this->buffer[] = $payload;
        $this->ensureFlushRegistered();
        // Workers are long-lived: flush eagerly so their activity streams live
        // instead of only at process end.
        if (PHP_SAPI === 'cli' && count($this->buffer) >= 1) {
            $this->flush();
        }
    }

    private function ensureFlushRegistered(): void
    {
        if ($this->registered) {
            return;
        }
        $this->registered = true;
        // Runs on normal end AND on dd()/exit() â€” the only reliable post-dd hook.
        register_shutdown_function(function (): void {
            if (function_exists('fastcgi_finish_request')) {
                @fastcgi_finish_request();
            }
            $this->flush();
        });
    }

    /** POST the buffered events to the EnvKit ingest server (best-effort). */
    public function flush(): void
    {
        if ($this->buffer === []) {
            return;
        }
        $url = getenv('ENVKIT_DEBUG_INGEST');
        $token = getenv('ENVKIT_DEBUG_TOKEN') ?: '';
        if ($url === false || $url === '') {
            $this->buffer = [];
            return;
        }
        $events = $this->buffer;
        $this->buffer = [];
        $root = function_exists('base_path') ? base_path() : getcwd();
        $body = json_encode([
            'site' => $this->site,
            'root' => $root,
            'events' => $events,
        ]);
        if ($body === false) {
            return;
        }
        $this->post((string) $url, $token, $body);
    }

    private function post(string $url, string $token, string $body): void
    {
        if (function_exists('curl_init')) {
            $ch = curl_init($url);
            curl_setopt_array($ch, [
                CURLOPT_POST => true,
                CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => $body,
                CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [
                    'Content-Type: application/json',
                    'X-EnvKit-Token: ' . $token,
                ],
                CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,
                CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 2,
                CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT => 1,
            ]);
            @curl_exec($ch);
            curl_close($ch);

            return;
        }
        // Fallback when ext-curl is unavailable.
        $ctx = stream_context_create(['http' => [
            'method' => 'POST',
            'header' => "Content-Type: application/json\r\nX-EnvKit-Token: {$token}\r\n",
            'content' => $body,
            'timeout' => 2,
        ]]);
        @file_get_contents($url, false, $ctx);
    }
}

<?php

/**
 * EnvKit Debug collector for Laravel. Captures dump()/dd(), SQL queries (with the
 * timing EnvKit uses for N+1 / slow-query detection), outgoing mail, rendered
 * views, dispatched events, queued jobs, and outgoing HTTP, then streams them to
 * the EnvKit Debug window. Inert unless EnvKit has enabled debug for this project
 * (see {@see Client::enabled()}), so it is safe to leave registered.
 *
 * Installed + removed by EnvKit (copied to app/EnvKit/, registered in
 * bootstrap/providers.php or config/app.php). Self-contained â€” only Laravel and
 * Symfony VarDumper (always present in a Laravel app) are assumed.
 */

namespace App\EnvKit;

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\View;
use Illuminate\Support\ServiceProvider;
use Symfony\Component\VarDumper\Cloner\VarCloner;
use Symfony\Component\VarDumper\Dumper\HtmlDumper;
use Symfony\Component\VarDumper\VarDumper;

class EnvKitDebugServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider
{
    private ?Client $client = null;

    public function boot(): void
    {
        $client = new Client($this->siteName());
        if (! $client->enabled()) {
            return;
        }
        // Queue-worker (CLI) capture is opt-in.
        if (PHP_SAPI === 'cli' && ! $client->captureWorkers()) {
            return;
        }
        $this->client = $client;

        // Tag every event with the request line ("GET /orders/42") so the Debug
        // window can group a request's signals under one header. Web SAPI only â€”
        // CLI workers have no request and group by job/worker instead.
        if (PHP_SAPI !== 'cli') {
            $method = isset($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD']) ? (string) $_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] : '';
            $uri = isset($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI']) ? (string) $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'] : '';
            $line = trim($method . ' ' . $uri);
            if ($line !== '') {
                $client->setRequest($line);
            }
        }

        $this->hookDumps($client);
        $this->hookQueries($client);
        $this->hookMail($client);
        $this->hookViews($client);
        $this->hookCache($client);
        $this->hookEvents($client);
        $this->hookJobs($client);
        $this->hookHttp($client);

        // Flush after the framework finishes (HTTP). dd()/exit are covered by the
        // shutdown function the client registers on first record().
        $this->app->terminating(static function () use ($client): void {
            $client->flush();
        });
    }

    private function siteName(): string
    {
        $dir = getenv('ENVKIT_DEBUG_DIR') ?: '';
        if ($dir !== '') {
            // The state file carries the EnvKit site name; cheaper to read here than
            // to plumb it through env.
            $root = function_exists('base_path') ? base_path() : getcwd();
            $key = substr(sha1(strtolower(rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', (string) $root), '/'))), 0, 16);
            $data = @file_get_contents(rtrim($dir, '/\\') . '/' . $key . '.json');
            if ($data !== false) {
                $parsed = json_decode($data, true);
                if (is_array($parsed) && isset($parsed['site'])) {
                    return (string) $parsed['site'];
                }
            }
        }
        try {
            return (string) config('app.name', '');
        } catch (\Throwable) {
            return '';
        }
    }

    /** Intercept dump()/dd() via VarDumper; render to HTML for the window. */
    private function hookDumps(Client $client): void
    {
        $cloner = new VarCloner();
        $htmlDumper = new HtmlDumper();
        $passthrough = $client->passthrough();

        VarDumper::setHandler(function ($var) use ($client, $cloner, $htmlDumper, $passthrough): void {
            $data = $cloner->cloneVar($var);
            $html = $htmlDumper->dump($data, true);
            $caller = $this->callerFrame();
            $client->record('dump', [
                'label' => 'dump',
                'html' => is_string($html) ? $html : '',
                'source' => $caller,
            ]);
            // Clean response unless passthrough: only echo inline when asked.
            if ($passthrough) {
                $htmlDumper->dump($data);
            }
        });
    }

    private function hookQueries(Client $client): void
    {
        DB::listen(static function ($query) use ($client): void {
            $client->record('query', [
                'label' => $query->sql,
                'durationMs' => (int) round((float) $query->time),
                'data' => [
                    'sql' => $query->sql,
                    'bindings' => array_map(static fn ($b) => is_scalar($b) || $b === null ? $b : (string) json_encode($b), $query->bindings),
                    'connection' => method_exists($query, 'getConnectionName') ? $query->getConnectionName() : ($query->connectionName ?? ''),
                    'timeMs' => (float) $query->time,
                ],
            ]);
        });
    }

    private function hookMail(Client $client): void
    {
        $event = '\\Illuminate\\Mail\\Events\\MessageSending';
        if (! class_exists($event)) {
            return;
        }
        Event::listen($event, static function ($e) use ($client): void {
            $message = $e->message ?? null; // Symfony\Component\Mime\Email
            $subject = '';
            $to = [];
            try {
                if ($message) {
                    $subject = method_exists($message, 'getSubject') ? (string) $message->getSubject() : '';
                    foreach ((method_exists($message, 'getTo') ? $message->getTo() : []) as $addr) {
                        $to[] = method_exists($addr, 'getAddress') ? $addr->getAddress() : (string) $addr;
                    }
                }
            } catch (\Throwable) {
                // best-effort
            }
            $client->record('mail', [
                'label' => $subject !== '' ? $subject : 'Mail',
                'data' => ['subject' => $subject, 'to' => $to],
            ]);
        });
    }

    private function hookViews(Client $client): void
    {
        View::composer('*', static function ($view) use ($client): void {
            $client->record('view', [
                'label' => $view->getName(),
                'data' => ['name' => $view->getName(), 'path' => $view->getPath()],
            ]);
        });
    }

    private function hookCache(Client $client): void
    {
        $kinds = [
            '\\Illuminate\\Cache\\Events\\CacheHit' => 'hit',
            '\\Illuminate\\Cache\\Events\\CacheMissed' => 'miss',
            '\\Illuminate\\Cache\\Events\\KeyWritten' => 'write',
            '\\Illuminate\\Cache\\Events\\KeyForgotten' => 'forget',
        ];
        foreach ($kinds as $event => $op) {
            if (! class_exists($event)) {
                continue;
            }
            Event::listen($event, static function ($e) use ($client, $op): void {
                $key = '';
                $store = '';
                try {
                    $key = isset($e->key) ? (string) $e->key : '';
                    $store = isset($e->storeName) ? (string) $e->storeName : '';
                } catch (\Throwable) {
                    // best-effort
                }
                $client->record('cache', [
                    'label' => $key !== '' ? $key : $op,
                    'data' => ['op' => $op, 'key' => $key, 'store' => $store],
                ]);
            });
        }
    }

    private function hookEvents(Client $client): void
    {
        Event::listen('*', static function (string $name, array $payload) use ($client): void {
            // Skip framework noise (and our own listeners) to keep the stream useful.
            if (str_starts_with($name, 'Illuminate\\') || str_starts_with($name, 'eloquent.')) {
                return;
            }
            $client->record('event', [
                'label' => $name,
                'data' => ['name' => $name, 'payloadCount' => count($payload)],
            ]);
        });
    }

    private function hookJobs(Client $client): void
    {
        try {
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Queue::before(static function ($e) use ($client): void {
                $client->record('job', [
                    'label' => self::jobName($e),
                    'data' => ['state' => 'processing', 'name' => self::jobName($e)],
                ]);
            });
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Queue::after(static function ($e) use ($client): void {
                $client->record('job', [
                    'label' => self::jobName($e),
                    'data' => ['state' => 'processed', 'name' => self::jobName($e)],
                ]);
            });
            \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Queue::failing(static function ($e) use ($client): void {
                $client->record('job', [
                    'label' => self::jobName($e),
                    'data' => ['state' => 'failed', 'name' => self::jobName($e)],
                ]);
            });
        } catch (\Throwable) {
            // Queue facade may be unavailable in some minimal apps.
        }
    }

    private static function jobName($e): string
    {
        try {
            if (isset($e->job) && method_exists($e->job, 'resolveName')) {
                return (string) $e->job->resolveName();
            }
        } catch (\Throwable) {
            // fall through
        }

        return 'job';
    }

    private function hookHttp(Client $client): void
    {
        // Laravel 10.14+ exposes global middleware on the HTTP client *factory*.
        // `Http` is a facade, so method_exists() on the facade CLASS is always
        // false (the method lives on the resolved Factory, reached via
        // __callStatic) â€” check the facade root instead, or HTTP never captures.
        $factory = \Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http::getFacadeRoot();
        if (! is_object($factory) || ! method_exists($factory, 'globalResponseMiddleware')) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            $factory->globalResponseMiddleware(static function ($response) use ($client) {
                try {
                    $req = $response->transferStats?->getRequest();
                    $uri = $req ? (string) $req->getUri() : '';
                    $method = $req ? $req->getMethod() : '';
                    $ms = $response->transferStats ? (float) $response->transferStats->getTransferTime() * 1000 : null;
                    $client->record('http', [
                        'label' => trim($method . ' ' . $uri),
                        'durationMs' => $ms !== null ? (int) round($ms) : null,
                        'data' => ['method' => $method, 'uri' => $uri, 'status' => $response->status()],
                    ]);
                } catch (\Throwable) {
                    // best-effort
                }

                return $response;
            });
        } catch (\Throwable) {
            // ignore â€” HTTP capture is best-effort
        }
    }

    /** Best-effort user-code frame for a dump (skip the collector + framework). */
    private function callerFrame(): array
    {
        foreach (debug_backtrace(DEBUG_BACKTRACE_IGNORE_ARGS, 30) as $frame) {
            $file = $frame['file'] ?? '';
            if ($file === '' || str_contains($file, 'EnvKit') || str_contains($file, 'var-dumper') || str_contains($file, '/vendor/')) {
                continue;
            }

            return ['file' => $file, 'line' => (int) ($frame['line'] ?? 0)];
        }

        return ['file' => '', 'line' => 0];
    }
}

    public function autoSaveSettings(): void
    {
        $settings = NotificationSetting::first() ?? NotificationSetting::current();
        $state = $this->form->getRawState();

        $offsets = array_map('intval', (array) ($state['reminder_offsets'] ?? [-3, 0, 2, 7]));
        $state['reminder_offsets'] = array_values(array_unique($offsets));

        $settings->update([
            'reminder_offsets' => $state['reminder_offsets'],
            'renewal_days_before' => (int) ($state['renewal_days_before'] ?? 15),
            'adaptive_timing_enabled' => (bool) ($state['adaptive_timing_enabled'] ?? true),
            'birthday_greeting_enabled' => (bool) ($state['birthday_greeting_enabled'] ?? true),
            'installment_template' => (string) ($state['installment_template'] ?? $settings->installment_template),
            'overdue_template' => (string) ($state['overdue_template'] ?? $settings->overdue_template),
            'renewal_template' => (string) ($state['renewal_template'] ?? $settings->renewal_template),
            'birthday_template' => (string) ($state['birthday_template'] ?? $settings->birthday_template),
        ]);

        NotificationSetting::clearCache();

        Notification::make()
            ->title('ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ±Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù‡ ØµÙˆØ±Øª Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ø´Ø¯.')
            ->success()
            ->duration(2000)
            ->send();
    }

    public function selectAllVisibleCustomers(): void
    {
        $visibleIds = $this->getFilteredCustomers()->pluck('id')->map(fn ($id) => (int) $id)->all();
        $this->custom_user_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_merge(
            array_map('intval', $this->custom_user_ids),
            $visibleIds
        )));
    }

    public function selectOverdueCustomers(): void
    {
        // Ù…Ø´Ú©Ù„ Ø®Ø·Ø§ÛŒ 500 Ø¯Ø± Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ù…ØªØ¯ Ø¨Ø§ Ø§ÙØ²ÙˆØ¯Ù† Ù¾ÛŒØ´ÙˆÙ†Ø¯ installments Ø¨Ù‡ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ø¨Ø±Ø·Ø±Ù Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª
        $overdueUserIds = Installment::where('installments.status', 'overdue')
            ->join('policies', 'installments.policy_id', '=', 'policies.id')
            ->pluck('policies.user_id')
            ->filter()
            ->map(fn ($id) => (int) $id)
            ->unique()
            ->values()
            ->all();

        $this->custom_user_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_merge(
            array_map('intval', $this->custom_user_ids),
            $overdueUserIds
        )));

        Notification::make()
            ->title(count($overdueUserIds) . ' Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø± Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù„ÛŒØ³Øª Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ Ø§Ø¶Ø§ÙÙ‡ Ø´Ø¯Ù†Ø¯.')
            ->info()
            ->send();
    }

    public function clearSelectedCustomers(): void
    {
        $this->custom_user_ids = [];
    }

    public function retryFailedLog(int $logId): void
    {
        $log = NotificationLog::find($logId);
        if ($log) {
            $log->update(['status' => 'queued', 'error_message' => null]);
            SendBaleMessageJob::dispatch($log->id);

            Notification::make()
                ->title('Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ù…Ø¬Ø¯Ø¯Ø§Ù‹ Ø¯Ø± ØµÙ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù„Ù‡ Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ú¯Ø±ÙØª.')
                ->success()
                ->send();
        }
    }

    public function sendCustomMessage(NotificationDispatcherService $dispatcher): void
    {
        $this->custom_user_ids = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(array_map('intval', $this->custom_user_ids))));

        $this->validate([
            'custom_user_ids' => ['required', 'array', 'min:1'],
            'custom_user_ids.*' => ['integer', 'exists:users,id'],
            'custom_message' => ['required', 'string', 'min:3'],
        ], [
            'custom_user_ids.required' => 'Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ù‚Ù„ ÛŒÚ© Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø± Ø±Ø§ Ø§Ø² Ù„ÛŒØ³Øª Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯.',
            'custom_message.required' => 'Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ù…ØªÙ† Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ø§Ø®ØªØµØ§ØµÛŒ Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯.',
        ]);

        $successCount = 0;

        User::whereIn('id', $this->custom_user_ids)
            ->chunkById(100, function ($users) use ($dispatcher, &$successCount): void {
                foreach ($users as $user) {
                    $dispatcher->dispatch(
                        $user,
                        'custom_message',
                        (int) $user->id,
                        random_int(1000, 999999),
                        trim($this->custom_message),
                        route('customer.dashboard')
                    );
                    $successCount++;
                }
            });

        if ($successCount === 0) {
            Notification::make()->title('Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.')->warning()->send();
            return;
        }

        SystemActivity::create([
            'actor_id' => auth()->id(),
            'event_type' => 'notification_sent',
            'title' => "Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ø§Ø®ØªØµØ§ØµÛŒ Ø¨Ù‡ {$successCount} Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø±",
            'icon' => 'app_notifications',
            'color' => 'indigo',
            'details' => [
                'description' => mb_substr(trim($this->custom_message), 0, 120),
            ],
        ]);

        $this->reset(['custom_user_ids', 'custom_message', 'customerSearch']);

        Notification::make()
            ->title("Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ø§Ø®ØªØµØ§ØµÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ {$successCount} Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø± Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯.")
            ->success()
            ->send();
    }

    protected function getFilteredCustomers()
    {
        $search = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText(trim($this->customerSearch));

        $customers = User::query()
            ->select(['id', 'name', 'mobile', 'national_code'])
            ->when($search !== '', function ($q) use ($search): void {
                $q->where(function ($sub) use ($search): void {
                    $sub->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                        ->orWhere('mobile', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                        ->orWhere('national_code', 'like', "%{$search}%");
                });
            })
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->limit(120)
            ->get();

        if (! empty($this->custom_user_ids)) {
            $selectedUsers = User::whereIn('id', $this->custom_user_ids)
                ->get(['id', 'name', 'mobile', 'national_code']);
            $customers = $selectedUsers->merge($customers)->unique('id')->values();
        }

        return $customers;
    }

    protected function getViewData(): array
    {
        $logs = NotificationLog::with('user:id,name,mobile')
            ->latest()
            ->limit(50)
            ->get();

        $activities = SystemActivity::whereIn('event_type', ['notification_sent', 'birthday_greeting', 'cron_job_executed'])
            ->latest()
            ->limit(5)
            ->get();

        return [
            'logs' => $logs,
            'activities' => $activities,
            'customers' => $this->getFilteredCustomers(),
        ];
    }
}

namespace App\Filament\Resources\InstallmentResource\Pages;

{
    protected static string $resource = InstallmentResource::class;

    protected function getHeaderActions(): array
    {
        return [
            Actions\CreateAction::make(),

            Actions\Action::make('importExcel')
                ->label('Ø§ÛŒÙ…Ù¾ÙˆØ±Øª ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„ Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø· / Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§')

                ->form([
                    Forms\Components\FileUpload::make('excel_file')
                        ->label('Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„ (.xls ÛŒØ§ .xlsx ÛŒØ§ .csv)')
                        ->disk('local')
                        ->directory('imports')
                        ->required(),
                ])

                    @set_time_limit(0);
                    @ini_set('memory_limit', '512M');

                    $relativePath = (string) ($data['excel_file'] ?? '');
                    $fullPath = Storage::disk('local')->path($relativePath);

                    try {
                        $stats = $importService->import($fullPath);

                        Notification::make()
                            ->title('Ù‡Ù…Ú¯Ø§Ù…â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø§Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ù… Ø´Ø¯.')

                                $stats['users_processed'] ?? 0,
                                $stats['policies_processed'] ?? 0,
                                $stats['installments_processed'] ?? 0
                            ))
                            ->success()
                            ->send();
                    } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                        Notification::make()
                            ->title('Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø²Ø´ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„')
                            ->body($e->getMessage())
                            ->danger()
                            ->send();
                    } finally {
                        if ($relativePath !== '' && Storage::disk('local')->exists($relativePath)) {
                            Storage::disk('local')->delete($relativePath);
                        }
                    }
                }),
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Filament\Resources\InsurerResource\Pages;

use App\Casts\JalaliDateCast;
use App\Filament\Resources\InsurerResource;
use App\Jobs\SendBaleMessageJob;
use App\Models\Installment;
use App\Models\NotificationLog;
use App\Models\NotificationSetting;
use App\Models\Policy;
use App\Services\NotificationDispatcherService;
use Filament\Actions;
use Filament\Notifications\Notification;
use Filament\Resources\Pages\ManageRecords;

class ManageInsurers extends ManageRecords
{
    protected static string $resource = InsurerResource::class;

    protected static string $view = 'filament.resources.insurer-resource.pages.manage-insurers';

    public string $serviceTab = 'smart_dispatch'; // smart_dispatch | bale_settings_logs | webapp_notifications

    public string $installmentTemplate = '';

    public string $overdueTemplate = '';

    public string $renewalTemplate = '';

    public int $renewalDaysBefore = 15;

    public function mount(): void
    {
        parent::mount();
        $settings = NotificationSetting::current();
        $this->installmentTemplate = $settings->installment_template;
        $this->overdueTemplate = $settings->overdue_template;
        $this->renewalTemplate = $settings->renewal_template;
        $this->renewalDaysBefore = $settings->renewal_days_before;
    }

    protected function getHeaderActions(): array
    {
        return [
            Actions\CreateAction::make()
                ->label('Ø§ÙØ²ÙˆØ¯Ù† Ø´Ø±Ú©Øª Ùˆ Ø³Ø±ÙˆÛŒØ³')
                ->icon('heroicon-o-plus')
                ->modalHeading('Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø±Ú©Øª Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡ Ùˆ Ù†ÙˆØ¹ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡'),
        ];
    }

    public function saveBaleSettings(): void
    {
        $settings = NotificationSetting::current();
        $settings->update([
            'installment_template' => $this->installmentTemplate,
            'overdue_template' => $this->overdueTemplate,
            'renewal_template' => $this->renewalTemplate,
            'renewal_days_before' => $this->renewalDaysBefore,
        ]);

        Notification::make()
            ->title('ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù„Ù‡ Ùˆ Ù‚Ø§Ù„Ø¨ Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù…â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ø´Ø¯.')
            ->success()
            ->send();
    }

    /**
     * Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± ØªÙ…Ø§Ù… Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø· Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚ Ø§Ø² Ø¯ÛŒØªØ§Ø¨ÛŒØ³ Ùˆ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ø§Ø®ØªØµØ§ØµÛŒ Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚Ø§Øª
     */
    public function triggerAutoOverdueMessages(): void
    {
        $overdueInstallments = Installment::with(['policy.user'])
            ->where('status', 'overdue')
            ->get();

        $settings = NotificationSetting::current();
        $dispatcher = app(NotificationDispatcherService::class);
        $sentCount = 0;

        foreach ($overdueInstallments as $inst) {
            $user = $inst->policy?->user;
            if (! $user) {
                continue;
            }

            $msg = str_replace(
                ['{name}', '{amount}', '{due_date}', '{policy_number}'],
                [
                    $user->name ?: $user->mobile,
                    number_format($inst->amount),
                    JalaliDateCast::toJalali($inst->due_date),
                    $inst->policy->policy_number,
                ],
                $settings->overdue_template
            );

            $dispatcher->dispatch(
                user: $user,
                type: 'overdue_auto_blast',
                relatedId: $inst->id,
                offsetDay: (int) now()->format('YmdHi'),
                message: $msg,
                channels: ['bale', 'database', 'webpush']
            );
            $sentCount++;
        }

        Notification::make()
            ->title("Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ø§Ø®Ø·Ø§Ø± Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚Ø§Øª Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ {$sentCount} Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ù…Ø¹ÙˆÙ‚ Ø¨Ù‡ ØµÙˆØ±Øª Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯.")
            ->success()
            ->send();
    }

    /**
     * Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± Ø¢Ø³ØªØ§Ù†Ù‡ Ø§Ù†Ù‚Ø¶Ø§ Ùˆ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ø§Ø®ØªØµØ§ØµÛŒ ØªÙ…Ø¯ÛŒØ¯
     */
    public function triggerAutoExpirationMessages(): void
    {
        $settings = NotificationSetting::current();
        $today = now('Asia/Tehran')->toDateString();
        $limit = now('Asia/Tehran')->addDays($settings->renewal_days_before)->toDateString();

        $expiringPolicies = Policy::with('user')
            ->whereNotNull('expires_at')
            ->whereBetween('expires_at', [$today, $limit])
            ->get();

        $dispatcher = app(NotificationDispatcherService::class);
        $sentCount = 0;

        foreach ($expiringPolicies as $policy) {
            $user = $policy->user;
            if (! $user) {
                continue;
            }

            $daysLeft = max(0, (int) now('Asia/Tehran')->startOfDay()->diffInDays($policy->expires_at, false));
            $msg = str_replace(
                ['{name}', '{policy_number}', '{title}', '{expires_at}', '{days_left}'],
                [
                    $user->name ?: $user->mobile,
                    $policy->policy_number,
                    $policy->vehicle_name ?: $policy->title,
                    JalaliDateCast::toJalali($policy->expires_at),
                    $daysLeft,
                ],
                $settings->renewal_template
            );

            $dispatcher->dispatch(
                user: $user,
                type: 'renewal_auto_blast',
                relatedId: $policy->id,
                offsetDay: (int) now()->format('YmdHi'),
                message: $msg,
                channels: ['bale', 'database', 'webpush']
            );
            $sentCount++;
        }

        Notification::make()
            ->title("Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ø¢ÙˆØ± Ø§Ù†Ù‚Ø¶Ø§ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ {$sentCount} Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡ Ø¨Ù‡ ØµÙˆØ±Øª Ø®ÙˆØ¯Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯.")
            ->success()
            ->send();
    }

    public function retryBaleLog(int $logId): void
    {
        $log = NotificationLog::find($logId);
        if ($log) {
            $log->update(['status' => 'queued', 'error_message' => null]);
            SendBaleMessageJob::dispatch($log->id);
            Notification::make()->title('Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ù…Ø¬Ø¯Ø¯Ø§Ù‹ Ø¯Ø± ØµÙ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù„Ù‡ Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ú¯Ø±ÙØª.')->success()->send();
        }
    }

    public function getBaleLogsProperty()
    {
        return NotificationLog::with('user')
            ->where('channel', 'bale')
            ->latest()
            ->limit(30)
            ->get();
    }

    public function getWebAppLogsProperty()
    {
        return NotificationLog::with('user')
            ->whereIn('channel', ['database', 'webpush'])
            ->latest()
            ->limit(30)
            ->get();
    }
}

                ->label('Ø§ÛŒÙ…Ù¾ÙˆØ±Øª Ù‡ÙˆØ´Ù…Ù†Ø¯ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„')

                ])
                ->action(function (array $data, ExcelImportService $importService): void {
                    @set_time_limit(0);
                    @ini_set('memory_limit', '512M');

                    $relativePath = (string) ($data['excel_file'] ?? '');

                    try {
                        $stats = $importService->import($fullPath);

                        Notification::make()
                            ->title('Ø§ÛŒÙ…Ù¾ÙˆØ±Øª Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø§Ù†Ø¬Ø§Ù… Ø´Ø¯.')

                            ->success()
                            ->send();
                    } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                        Notification::make()
                            ->title('Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø²Ø´ ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„')
                            ->body($e->getMessage())
                            ->danger()
                            ->send();

                            Storage::disk('local')->delete($relativePath);
                        }
                    }
                }),
        ];
    }
}

    public function saveProfile(): void
    {
        /** @var User $user */
        $user = $this->getRecord();

        $name = PersianNormalizer::normalize((string) ($this->editData['name'] ?? ''));
        $mobile = PersianNormalizer::cleanMobile((string) ($this->editData['mobile'] ?? ''));

        if ($name === '' || $mobile === '') {
            Notification::make()
                ->title('Ù†Ø§Ù… Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø± Ùˆ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ ØªÙ„ÙÙ† Ù‡Ù…Ø±Ø§Ù‡ Ø§Ù„Ø²Ø§Ù…ÛŒ Ø§Ø³Øª.')
                ->danger()
                ->send();
            return;

            ? JalaliDateCast::toGregorian((string) $this->editData['birth_date'])
            : null;

        $updatePayload = [
            'name'              => $name,
            'national_code'     => PersianNormalizer::cleanNationalCode((string) ($this->editData['national_code'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'mobile'            => $mobile,
            'phone'             => PersianNormalizer::normalize((string) ($this->editData['phone'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'birth_date'        => $birthDateGregorian,
            'policyholder_type' => $this->editData['policyholder_type'] ?? 'Ø­Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚ÛŒ',
            'profile_title'     => PersianNormalizer::normalize((string) ($this->editData['profile_title'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'payment_badge'     => $this->editData['payment_badge'] ?? 'normal',
            'bale_chat_id'      => trim((string) ($this->editData['bale_chat_id'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'address'           => trim((string) ($this->editData['address'] ?? '')) ?: null,
        ];

        if (! empty($this->editData['new_password'])) {
            $updatePayload['password'] = Hash::make((string) $this->editData['new_password']);
        }

        $user->update($updatePayload);
        $this->record = $user->fresh(['policies.insurer', 'policies.installments']);
        $this->isEditing = false;

        Notification::make()
            ->title('Ø§Ø·Ù„Ø§Ø¹Ø§Øª Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÙ†Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø± Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø¨Ø±ÙˆØ²Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯.')

    public function openCreatePolicyModal(): void

        /** @var User $user */
        $user = $this->getRecord();
        $defaultInsurerId = Insurer::first()?->id;

        $this->editingPolicyId = null;
        $this->policyFormData = [
            'policy_number'         => '',
            'insurer_id'            => $defaultInsurerId,
            'title'                 => 'Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡ Ø´Ø®Øµ Ø«Ø§Ù„Ø«',
            'insurance_category'    => 'Ø«Ø§Ù„Ø«',
            'vehicle_name'          => '',
            'plate_number'          => '',
            'vehicle_model'         => '',
            'no_claim_years'        => '',
            'driver_no_claim_years' => '',
            'profile_title'         => $user->profile_title ?? '',

    public function deletePolicy(int $policyId): void
    {

    public function approveInstallment(int $installmentId): void
    {

    public function saveInstallment(): void
    {
        if (! $this->editingInstallmentId) {
            return;
        }

        /** @var User $user */
        $user = $this->getRecord();

        $installment = Installment::whereHas('policy', fn ($q) => $q->where('user_id', $user->id))
            ->findOrFail($this->editingInstallmentId);

        $dueJalali = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText((string) ($this->installmentFormData['due_date_jalali'] ?? ''));
        $dueGregorian = JalaliDateCast::toGregorian($dueJalali) ?? ($installment->due_date ? $installment->due_date->toDateString() : now('Asia/Tehran')->toDateString());
        $amount = (int) PersianNormalizer::cleanNumber($this->installmentFormData['amount'] ?? $installment->amount);
        $docAmount = (int) PersianNormalizer::cleanNumber($this->installmentFormData['documented_amount'] ?? 0);
        $status = (string) ($this->installmentFormData['status'] ?? 'pending');

        $installment->update([
            'installment_number' => max(1, (int) ($this->installmentFormData['installment_number'] ?? 1)),
            'amount'             => $amount,
            'documented_amount'  => $docAmount > 0 ? $docAmount : ($status === 'paid' ? $amount : null),
            'paid_amount'        => $status === 'paid' ? ($docAmount > 0 ? $docAmount : $amount) : 0,
            'due_date_jalali'    => $dueJalali ?: null,
            'due_date'           => $dueGregorian,
            'payment_identifier' => PersianNormalizer::cleanNumber($this->installmentFormData['payment_identifier'] ?? '') ?: null,
            'status'             => $status,
            'paid_at'            => $status === 'paid' ? ($installment->paid_at ?? now()) : null,
        ]);

        $this->record = $user->fresh(['policies.insurer', 'policies.installments']);
        $this->closeInstallmentFormModal();

        Notification::make()
            ->title('Ø§Ø·Ù„Ø§Ø¹Ø§Øª Ù‚Ø³Ø· Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø¨Ø±ÙˆØ²Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯.')
            ->success()
            ->send();
    }

    public function retryNotification(int $logId): void
    {
        /** @var User $user */
        $user = $this->getRecord();

        $log = NotificationLog::where('user_id', $user->id)->find($logId);
        if ($log) {
            $log->update(['status' => 'queued', 'error_message' => null]);
            SendBaleMessageJob::dispatch($log->id);

            Notification::make()
                ->title('Ù¾ÛŒØ§Ù… Ù…Ø¬Ø¯Ø¯Ø§Ù‹ Ø¯Ø± ØµÙ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø±Ø¨Ø§Øª Ø¨Ù„Ù‡ Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ú¯Ø±ÙØª.')
                ->success()
                ->send();
        }
    }

    protected function getViewData(): array
    {
        /** @var User $user */
        $user = $this->getRecord()->loadMissing(['policies.insurer', 'policies.installments']);
        $today = now('Asia/Tehran')->startOfDay();

        $allPolicies = $user->policies ?? collect();
        $allInstallments = $allPolicies->flatMap(function (Policy $policy) {
            return $policy->installments->each(function (Installment $inst) use ($policy): void {
                $inst->setRelation('policy', $policy);
            });
        });

        // Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø³Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚ Ø®Ù„Ø§ØµÙ‡ Ù…Ø§Ù„ÛŒ (Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø§Ø² Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª Ù†Ù‚Ø¯ÛŒ Ú©Ù„ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡ + Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙ¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª + Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø·)
        $paidFromInstallments = (int) $allInstallments->where('status', 'paid')->sum('amount');
        $paidFromCashAndInitial = (int) $allPolicies->sum(function (Policy $p): int {
            $isCash = $p->payment_type === 'cash'
                || ($p->installments->isEmpty() && $p->total_amount > 0 && ($p->initial_payment >= $p->total_amount || $p->paid_amount >= $p->total_amount));

            if ($isCash) {
                return max((int) $p->total_amount, (int) $p->paid_amount, (int) $p->initial_payment);
            }

            return (int) $p->initial_payment;
        });

        $paidInstallmentsAmount = $paidFromInstallments + $paidFromCashAndInitial;
        $overdueInstallments = $allInstallments->where('status', 'overdue');
        $overdueCount = $overdueInstallments->count();
        $overdueAmount = (int) $overdueInstallments->sum('amount');
        $pendingAmount = (int) $allInstallments->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'overdue', 'awaiting_approval'])->sum('amount');

        // Û±. ÙÛŒÙ„ØªØ± Ùˆ Ø¬Ø³ØªØ¬ÙˆÛŒ Ø¨ÛŒÙ…Ù‡â€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù…Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§
        $policySearchTerm = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText(trim($this->policySearch));
        $filteredPolicies = $allPolicies->filter(function (Policy $policy) use ($policySearchTerm, $today): bool {
            if ($policySearchTerm !== '') {
                $haystack = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText(implode(' ', [
                    $policy->policy_number,
                    $policy->title,
                    $policy->vehicle_name,
                    $policy->plate_number,
                    $policy->insurer?->name,
                    $policy->profile_title,
                ]));
                if (! str_contains(mb_strtolower($haystack), mb_strtolower($policySearchTerm))) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            $isCash = $policy->payment_type === 'cash'
                || ($policy->installments->isEmpty() && $policy->total_amount > 0);

            if ($this->policyPaymentFilter === 'cash' && ! $isCash) {
                return false;
            }
            if ($this->policyPaymentFilter === 'installment' && $isCash) {
                return false;
            }

            if ($this->policyStatusFilter !== 'all') {
                if (! $policy->expires_at) {
                    return $this->policyStatusFilter === 'active';
                }
                $daysLeft = (int) $today->diffInDays($policy->expires_at->copy()->startOfDay(), false);

                if ($this->policyStatusFilter === 'expired' && $daysLeft >= 0) {
                    return false;
                }
                if ($this->policyStatusFilter === 'expiring_soon' && ($daysLeft < 0 || $daysLeft > 30)) {
                    return false;
                }
                if ($this->policyStatusFilter === 'active' && $daysLeft < 0) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        })->values();

        // Û². ÙÛŒÙ„ØªØ±ØŒ Ø¬Ø³ØªØ¬Ùˆ Ùˆ Ù…Ø±ØªØ¨â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ø§Ù‚Ø³Ø§Ø·
        $instSearchTerm = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText(trim($this->installmentSearch));
        $filteredInstallments = $allInstallments->filter(function (Installment $inst) use ($instSearchTerm): bool {
            if ($this->installmentStatusFilter !== 'all' && $inst->status !== $this->installmentStatusFilter) {
                return false;
            }

            if ($instSearchTerm !== '') {
                $haystack = PersianNormalizer::normalizeText(implode(' ', [
                    $inst->policy?->policy_number,
                    $inst->policy?->vehicle_name,
                    $inst->payment_identifier,
                    (string) $inst->amount,
                    $inst->due_date_jalali,
                ]));
                if (! str_contains(mb_strtolower($haystack), mb_strtolower($instSearchTerm))) {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            return true;
        });

        $filteredInstallments = match ($this->installmentSort) {
            'due_date_desc' => $filteredInstallments->sortByDesc('due_date')->values(),
            'amount_desc'   => $filteredInstallments->sortByDesc('amount')->values(),
            'number_asc'    => $filteredInstallments->sortBy('installment_number')->values(),
            default         => $filteredInstallments->sortBy('due_date')->values(),
        };

        // Û³. Ø¯Ø§Ø¯Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ¨ ØªØ§Ø±ÛŒØ®Ú†Ù‡ Ùˆ Ø§Ø±ØªØ¨Ø§Ø·Ø§Øª
        $notificationLogs = NotificationLog::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->latest()
            ->limit(40)
            ->get();

        $installmentIds = $allInstallments->pluck('id')->all();
        $audits = ! empty($installmentIds)
            ? InstallmentAudit::with(['actor:id,name', 'installment.policy:id,policy_number'])
                ->whereIn('installment_id', $installmentIds)
                ->latest()
                ->limit(40)
                ->get()
            : collect();

        $viewingPolicy = $this->viewingPolicyId
            ? $allPolicies->firstWhere('id', $this->viewingPolicyId)
            : null;

        $insurers = Insurer::pluck('name', 'id')->all();

        $notificationTargetUrl = NotificationSettingsPage::getUrl(['users' => (string) $user->id]) . '#custom-message-section';

        return [
            'user'                    => $user,
            'policies'                => $filteredPolicies,
            'totalPoliciesCount'      => $allPolicies->count(),
            'allInstallments'         => $filteredInstallments,
            'totalInstallmentsCount'  => $allInstallments->count(),
            'paidInstallmentsAmount'  => $paidInstallmentsAmount,
            'paidFromInstallments'    => $paidFromInstallments,
            'paidFromCashAndInitial'  => $paidFromCashAndInitial,
            'overdueCount'            => $overdueCount,
            'overdueAmount'           => $overdueAmount,
            'pendingAmount'           => $pendingAmount,
            'notificationLogs'        => $notificationLogs,
            'audits'                  => $audits,
            'historyTotalCount'       => $notificationLogs->count() + $audits->count(),
            'viewingPolicy'           => $viewingPolicy,
            'insurers'                => $insurers,
            'notificationTargetUrl'   => $notificationTargetUrl,
            'lastUpdatedJalali'       => JalaliDateCast::toJalali($user->updated_at, 'Y/m/d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Filament\Widgets;

use App\Models\Installment;
use App\Models\NotificationLog;
use App\Models\Policy;
use App\Models\SystemActivity;
use Filament\Widgets\Widget;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;
use Morilog\Jalali\Jalalian;

class RecentChangesWidget extends Widget
{
    public const CACHE_KEY = 'bimeh_dashboard_recent_changes';

    protected static string $view = 'filament.widgets.recent-changes-widget';

    protected int | string | array $columnSpan = 'full';

    public static function clearCache(): void
    {
        Cache::forget(self::CACHE_KEY);
    }

    protected function getViewData(): array
    {
        $cachedMetrics = Cache::remember(self::CACHE_KEY, now()->addMinutes(5), function (): array {
            $today = now('Asia/Tehran')->startOfDay();

            $logsQuery = NotificationLog::query()->whereDate('created_at', $today);
            if ((clone $logsQuery)->count() === 0) {
                $logsQuery = NotificationLog::query();
            }

            $totalLogsCount = (clone $logsQuery)->count();

            if ($totalLogsCount > 0) {
                $totalNotifiedUsers = (clone $logsQuery)->distinct('user_id')->count('user_id');

                $todayDueNotified = (clone $logsQuery)
                    ->where('type', 'installment_reminder')
                    ->where('offset_day', 0)
                    ->count();

                $twoDaysNotified = (clone $logsQuery)
                    ->where('type', 'installment_reminder')
                    ->whereIn('offset_day', [-2, 2])
                    ->count();

                $sevenDaysNotified = (clone $logsQuery)
                    ->where('type', 'installment_reminder')
                    ->whereIn('offset_day', [-7, 7])
                    ->count();

                $overdueNotified = (clone $logsQuery)
                    ->where('type', 'installment_reminder')
                    ->where('offset_day', '>', 0)
                    ->count();

                $expiring10DaysNotified = (clone $logsQuery)
                    ->where('type', 'policy_renewal')
                    ->count();
            } else {
                $todayDueNotified = Installment::whereDate('due_date', $today)->count();
                $twoDaysNotified = Installment::whereDate('due_date', $today->copy()->addDays(2))->count();
                $sevenDaysNotified = Installment::whereDate('due_date', $today->copy()->addDays(7))->count();

                $overdueNotified = Installment::where(function ($q) use ($today): void {
                    $q->where('status', 'overdue')
                        ->orWhere(function ($q2) use ($today): void {
                            $q2->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'awaiting_approval'])
                                ->whereDate('due_date', '<', $today);
                        });
                })->count();

                $expiring10DaysNotified = Policy::whereBetween('expires_at', [
                    $today->toDateString(),
                    $today->copy()->addDays(10)->toDateString(),
                ])->count();

                $totalNotifiedUsers = $todayDueNotified + $twoDaysNotified + $sevenDaysNotified + $overdueNotified + $expiring10DaysNotified;
            }

            $notificationReport = [
                'total_users'            => $totalNotifiedUsers,
                'today_count'            => $todayDueNotified,
                'two_days_count'         => $twoDaysNotified,
                'seven_days_count'       => $sevenDaysNotified,
                'overdue_count'          => $overdueNotified,
                'expiring_10_days_count' => $expiring10DaysNotified,
            ];

            $lastExcelActivity = SystemActivity::where('event_type', 'excel_imported')
                ->latest()
                ->first();

            $excelReport = null;

            if ($lastExcelActivity) {
                $details = $lastExcelActivity->details ?? [];
                $stats = $details['stats'] ?? [];

                $newUsers = (int) ($stats['new_users_count'] ?? $details['created_count'] ?? 0);
                $updatedUsers = (int) ($stats['updated_users_count'] ?? $details['updated_count'] ?? 0);
                $updatedPolicies = (int) ($stats['updated_policies_count'] ?? 0);
                $updatedInstallments = (int) ($stats['updated_installments_count'] ?? 0);

                $excelReport = [
                    'id'                                      => $lastExcelActivity->id,
                    'file_name'                               => $details['file_type'] ?? 'ÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ø§Ú©Ø³Ù„',
                    'time_jalali'                             => Jalalian::fromCarbon($lastExcelActivity->created_at->timezone('Asia/Tehran'))->format('Y/m/d H:i'),
                    'new_users_count'                         => $newUsers,
                    'updated_users_count'                     => $updatedUsers,
                    'updated_policies_count'                  => $updatedPolicies,
                    'updated_installments_count'              => $updatedInstallments,
                    'updated_policies_or_installments_count'  => $updatedPolicies + $updatedInstallments,
                    'status_changed_installments_count'       => $updatedInstallments,
                ];
            }

            return [
                'notificationReport' => $notificationReport,
                'excelReport'        => $excelReport,
                'excelReports'       => $excelReport ? [$excelReport] : [],
            ];
        });

        return array_merge($cachedMetrics, [
            'todayJalaliText'    => Jalalian::now()->format('l j F Y'),
            'todayJalaliNumeric' => Jalalian::now()->format('Y/m/d'),
        ]);
    }
}

    private function handleCallbackQuery(array $callbackQuery, BaleBotService $baleBot): JsonResponse
    {
        $callbackId = (string) ($callbackQuery['id'] ?? '');
        $data = (string) ($callbackQuery['data'] ?? '');
        $message = $callbackQuery['message'] ?? [];
        $chatId = (string) ($message['chat']['id'] ?? '');
        $messageId = (int) ($message['message_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($chatId === '' || $data === '') {
            return response()->json(['ok' => true]);
        }

        if (str_starts_with($data, 'confirm_pay:')) {
            $installmentId = (int) str_replace('confirm_pay:', '', $data);
            $user = User::where('bale_chat_id', $chatId)->first();
            $installment = Installment::with('policy')->find($installmentId);

            if (! $user || ! $installment || $installment->policy?->user_id !== $user->id) {
                $baleBot->answerCallbackQuery($callbackId, 'Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ù‚Ø³Ø· ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯ ÛŒØ§ Ù…ØªØ¹Ù„Ù‚ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª.', true);

                return response()->json(['ok' => true]);
            }

            if ($installment->status !== 'paid') {
                $installment->update([
                    'status' => 'awaiting_approval',
                    'followed_up_at' => now(),
                ]);
            }

            $baleBot->answerCallbackQuery($callbackId, 'âœ… Ø¨Ø§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯ Ùˆ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø± ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ø¯Ù…ÛŒÙ† Ù‚Ø±Ø§Ø± Ú¯Ø±ÙØª.');

            if ($messageId > 0) {
                $updatedText = ($message['text'] ?? '')
                    ."\n\nâœ… ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª Ø¬Ø¯ÛŒØ¯: Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù… Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø«Ø¨Øª Ø´Ø¯ Ùˆ Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ù†ØªØ¸Ø§Ø± ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ù†Ù‡Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ø§Ø¯Ù…ÛŒÙ† Ø§Ø³Øª.";
                $baleBot->editMessageText($chatId, $messageId, $updatedText);
            }

            return response()->json(['ok' => true]);
        }

        if (str_starts_with($data, 'reply_user:')) {
            $targetUserId = (int) str_replace('reply_user:', '', $data);
            $targetUser = User::find($targetUserId);

            if ($targetUser) {
                Cache::put("bale_state:{$chatId}", "admin_reply:{$targetUser->id}", now()->addMinutes(30));

                    null,
                    $baleBot->cancelSupportKeyboard()
                );
            }

            return response()->json(['ok' => true]);
        }

        return response()->json(['ok' => true]);
    }

    private function findUserByDigits(string $input): ?User
    {
        $cleanDigits = preg_replace('/\D+/', '', PersianNormalizer::normalize($input)) ?? '';
        if (strlen($cleanDigits) < 8 || strlen($cleanDigits) > 14) {
            return null;
        }

        $normalizedNational = PersianNormalizer::cleanNationalCode($cleanDigits);
        $normalizedMobile = PersianNormalizer::cleanMobile($cleanDigits);

        return User::where('national_code', $cleanDigits)
            ->orWhere('national_code', $normalizedNational)
            ->orWhere('mobile', $cleanDigits)
            ->orWhere('mobile', $normalizedMobile)
            ->first();
    }

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Customer;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Customer\CustomerLoginRequest;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\PersianNormalizer;
use Illuminate\Contracts\View\View;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class CustomerAuthController extends Controller
{
    public function showLoginForm(): View|RedirectResponse
    {
        if (Auth::check()) {
            return redirect()->route('customer.dashboard');
        }

        return view('customer.login');
    }

    public function login(CustomerLoginRequest $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $nationalCode = PersianNormalizer::cleanNationalCode((string) $request->input('national_code'));
        $mobileInput = PersianNormalizer::cleanMobile((string) $request->input('mobile'));

        $trimmedCode = ltrim($nationalCode, '0');

        if (strlen($nationalCode) < 8 || $trimmedCode === '' || strlen($mobileInput) < 10) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'national_code' => 'Ú©Ø¯ Ù…Ù„ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù…ÙˆØ¨Ø§ÛŒÙ„ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª.',
            ]);
        }

        $user = User::where('national_code', $nationalCode)
            ->orWhere('national_code', str_pad($nationalCode, 10, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT))
            ->when(strlen($trimmedCode) >= 8, fn ($q) => $q->orWhere('national_code', $trimmedCode))
            ->first();

        $isValid = $user && (
            $user->mobile === $mobileInput ||
            Hash::check($mobileInput, (string) $user->password)
        );

        if (! $isValid) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'national_code' => 'Ú©Ø¯ Ù…Ù„ÛŒ ÛŒØ§ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù…ÙˆØ¨Ø§ÛŒÙ„ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¯Ø± Ø³Ø§Ù…Ø§Ù†Ù‡ ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù†Ø´Ø¯.',
            ]);
        }

        Auth::login($user, $request->boolean('remember'));
        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->intended(route('customer.dashboard'));
    }

    public function logout(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        Auth::logout();
        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect()->route('customer.login');
    }
}

    protected function getCommonPortalData(User $user): array
    {

            ->whereIn('status', ['pending', 'overdue'])
            ->sortBy('due_date')

            ->whereIn('status', ['paid', 'awaiting_approval'])
            ->sortByDesc('updated_at')
            ->values();

            'user'                 => $user,
            'policies'             => $policies,
            'unreadCount'          => $user->unreadNotifications()->count(),
            'notifications'        => $user->notifications()->latest()->limit(20)->get(),
            'upcomingCount'        => $upcomingThreeMonths->count(),
            'upcomingTotal'        => (int) $upcomingThreeMonths->sum('amount'),
            'upcomingInstallments' => $upcomingInstallments,
            'paymentHistory'       => $paymentHistory,
            'baleBotUrl'           => $baleBotUrl,

                }

            } elseif ($hasInstallment) {
                $state = 'yellow';
            }

            $days[] = [
                'day'             => $d,
                'is_today'        => $isToday,
                'has_installment' => $hasInstallment,
                'state'           => $state,
                'installments'    => $dayInsts,
            ];
        }

        return [
            'year'              => $jYear,
            'month'             => $jMonth,
            'month_name'        => $monthNames[$jMonth] ?? '',
            'today_day'         => $jDay,
            'today_full_jalali' => $todayJalaliStr,
            'start_offset'      => $startDayOfWeek,
            'days_in_month'     => $daysInMonth,
            'days'              => $days,
        ];
    }

    protected function isJalaliLeapYear(int $jy): bool
    {
        $rem = ($jy + 2346) % 2820;
        return (((($rem + 38) * 31) % 128) < 31);
    }
}

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class SecurityHeadersMiddleware
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        /** @var Response $response */
        $response = $next($request);

        // ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ… Ù‡Ø¯Ø± Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø´ØªÛŒØ¨Ø§Ù†ÛŒ Ø§Ø² Ù…ÛŒÙ†ÛŒâ€ŒØ§Ù¾ Ø¨Ù„Ù‡ (Ø§Ø¬Ø§Ø²Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø±Ú¯Ø°Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± iframe Ø¨Ù„Ù‡)
        $response->headers->set('Content-Security-Policy', "frame-ancestors 'self' https://*.bale.ai;");
        $response->headers->set('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
        $response->headers->set('X-XSS-Protection', '1; mode=block');
        $response->headers->set('Referrer-Policy', 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
        $response->headers->set('Permissions-Policy', 'geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=()');

        return $response;
    }
}

namespace App\Http\Requests\Customer;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;

class CustomerLoginRequest extends FormRequest
{
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    protected function prepareForValidation(): void
    {
        $nationalCode = $this->normalizeDigits((string) $this->input('national_code', ''));
        $mobile = $this->normalizeDigits((string) $this->input('mobile', ''));

        // Ø§Ú¯Ø± Ú©Ø¯ Ù…Ù„ÛŒ Û¸ ÛŒØ§ Û¹ Ø±Ù‚Ù…ÛŒ (Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† ØµÙØ± Ø§ÙˆÙ„) ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯ØŒ Ø¨Ù‡ Û±Û° Ø±Ù‚Ù… Ø§Ø³ØªØ§Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯ ØªØ¨Ø¯ÛŒÙ„ Ø´ÙˆØ¯
        if (in_array(strlen($nationalCode), [8, 9], true)) {

        }

        // Ø§Ú¯Ø± Ù…ÙˆØ¨Ø§ÛŒÙ„ Û±Û° Ø±Ù‚Ù…ÛŒ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† ØµÙØ± Ø§ÙˆÙ„ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø´Ø¯ØŒ ØµÙØ± Ø§ÙˆÙ„ Ø§Ø¶Ø§ÙÙ‡ Ø´ÙˆØ¯
        if (strlen($mobile) === 10 && str_starts_with($mobile, '9')) {

            'national_code' => $nationalCode,
            'mobile' => $mobile,
        ]);
    }

    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'national_code' => ['required', 'string', 'min:8', 'max:15'],
            'mobile' => ['required', 'string', 'min:8', 'max:20'],
            'remember' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
        ];
    }

    public function messages(): array
    {
        return [
            'national_code.required' => 'Ù„Ø·ÙØ§Ù‹ Ú©Ø¯ Ù…Ù„ÛŒ Ø®ÙˆØ¯ Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯.',

        ];
    }

    private function normalizeDigits(string $value): string
    {
